Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on post-quantum structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 541. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 97 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 541 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the post-quantum structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 97. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
